1: // Clear priced negative stock batches and re-book the inventory correction.
2: //
3: //   npx tsx scripts/fix-negative-stock.ts --local                     # dry run
4: //   npx tsx scripts/fix-negative-stock.ts --local data/erp.sqlite --apply
5: //   ... --date 2026-11-19
6: //
7: // Four steps, one transaction:
8: //   1. Reverse the old OPENING-STOCK-CORR / PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR pair (posted by
9: //      post-periodic-stock-correction.ts). Those are the only manual corrections;
10: //      the app's own manufacturing/reversal entries are never touched.
11: //   2. Bring every *priced* negative batch to 0 and record the missing receipt
12: //      as an OPENING movement. Unpriced batches are left alone — there is no
13: //      value to book for them.
14: //   3. Dr 1200/1210/1220 / Cr 3100 for the opening value just added.
15: //   4. Dr 5000 COGS / Cr 1200 + 1210 so each raw/packing account lands on the
16: //      counted batch value. Finished goods are NOT trued up here — that needs a
17: //      physical count (see P0).
18: //
19: // Re-running is safe: every entry carries a fixed reference number and the
20: // whole run is skipped once OPENING-STOCK-CORR-2 exists.
21: 
22: import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
23: import type { JournalLineInput } from "../server/src/services/accountingService.js";
24: 
25: const OLD_REFS = ["OPENING-STOCK-CORR", "PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR"];
26: const REV_REFS = ["REV-OPENING-STOCK-CORR", "REV-PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR"];
27: const OPENING_REF = "OPENING-STOCK-CORR-2";
28: const ADJUST_REF = "PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR-2";
29: const MAX_ADJUSTMENT = 6_000_000;
30: 
31: const args = process.argv.slice(2);
32: const apply = args.includes("--apply");
33: const force = args.includes("--force");
34: const dateArg = args.indexOf("--date");
35: const entryDate = dateArg >= 0 ? args[dateArg + 1] : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
36: 
37: const localArg = args.indexOf("--local");
38: const useLocal = localArg >= 0;
39: if (useLocal) {
40:   process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = "local";
41:   const target = args[localArg + 1];
42:   if (target && !target.startsWith("--")) process.env.ERP_LOCAL_DB = target;
43: }
44: 
45: /** Batch cost when it has one, else the item's purchase price. */
46: const UNIT_COST = "COALESCE(NULLIF(sb.purchase_price,0), i.purchase_price, 0)";
47: 
48: /** Inventory account per item type — mirrors INVENTORY_ACCOUNT_BY_TYPE. */
49: const ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE: Record<string, string> = {
50:   RAW_MATERIAL: "1200",
51:   PACKING_MATERIAL: "1210",
52:   FINISHED_GOOD: "1220",
53: };
54: /** Types the periodic true-up may touch (finished goods wait for the count). */
55: const TRUE_UP_TYPES = ["RAW_MATERIAL", "PACKING_MATERIAL"];
56: 
57: type NegativeBatch = {
58:   id: number;
59:   item_id: number;
60:   warehouse_id: number;
61:   item_type: string;
62:   batch_number: string;
63:   quantity: number;
64:   price: number;
65: };
66: 
67: async function main(): Promise<void> {
68:   const { getDb } = await import("../server/src/db/index.js");
69:   const { env } = await import("../server/src/env.js");
70:   const { AccountingService } = await import("../server/src/services/accountingService.js");
71: 
72:   const db = getDb();
73:   console.log(
74:     `Database: ${
75:       useLocal ? `${env.localDbPath} (local — the service must be stopped)` : "(cloud)"
76:     }  date: ${entryDate}`,
77:   );
78: 
79:   const existing = new Set(
80:     (
81:       await db.all<{ reference_no: string }>(
82:         "SELECT reference_no FROM journal_entries WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no IS NOT NULL",
83:       )
84:     ).map((r) => r.reference_no),
85:   );
86:   if (existing.has(OPENING_REF)) {
87:     console.log(`${OPENING_REF} already posted — nothing to do.`);
88:     return;
89:   }
90: 
91:   const accountIds = new Map<string, number>();
92:   for (const row of await db.all<{ id: number; account_code: string }>(
93:     "SELECT id, account_code FROM accounts WHERE company_id = 1 AND account_code IN ('1200','1210','1220','3100','5000')",
94:   )) {
95:     accountIds.set(row.account_code, row.id);
96:   }
97:   const account = (code: string): number => {
98:     const id = accountIds.get(code);
99:     if (!id) throw new Error(`Account ${code} not found.`);
100:     return id;
101:   };
102: 
103:   // --- 1. reversal of the old correction pair -------------------------------
104:   type Job = { ref: string; narration: string; lines: JournalLineInput[]; total: number };
105:   const jobs: Job[] = [];
106: 
107:   for (let i = 0; i < OLD_REFS.length; i += 1) {
108:     if (existing.has(REV_REFS[i])) continue;
109:     const entry = await db.get<{ id: number; reference_no: string; narration: string | null }>(
110:       "SELECT id, reference_no, narration FROM journal_entries WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no = ?",
111:       [OLD_REFS[i]],
112:     );
113:     if (!entry) continue;
114:     const lines = await db.all<{ account_id: number; debit: number; credit: number; description: string | null }>(
115:       "SELECT account_id, debit, credit, description FROM journal_entry_lines WHERE journal_entry_id = ? ORDER BY id",
116:       [entry.id],
117:     );
118:     const total = lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Number(l.debit), 0);
119:     jobs.push({
120:       ref: REV_REFS[i],
121:       narration: `Reversal - ${entry.reference_no}`,
122:       total,
123:       lines: lines.map((l) => ({
124:         accountId: l.account_id,
125:         ...(Number(l.credit) > 0 ? { debit: Number(l.credit) } : { credit: Number(l.debit) }),
126:         description: `Reversal - ${l.description ?? entry.reference_no}`,
127:       })),
128:     });
129:   }
130: 
131:   // --- 2. priced negative batches -> 0 -------------------------------------
132:   const negatives = await db.all<NegativeBatch>(
133:     `SELECT sb.id, sb.item_id, sb.warehouse_id, i.item_type, sb.batch_number,

1: // Price unpriced opening batches from what the item actually cost on a
2: // purchase invoice. Reads the latest purchase line for the item (by invoice
3: // date) and copies its unit cost onto the batch, which is exactly what
4: // receiving a purchase does. Dry run unless --apply is passed.
5: //
6: //   node scripts/price-batches.mjs                       (dry run, packing)
7: //   node scripts/price-batches.mjs --type FINISHED_GOOD  (dry run, finished goods)
8: //   node scripts/price-batches.mjs --apply
9: //
10: // Options: --db <path>  (default: ERP_LOCAL_DB from .env)
11: 
12: import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
13: import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
14: 
15: const args = process.argv.slice(2);
16: const flag = (name, fallback) => {
17:   const i = args.indexOf(`--${name}`);
18:   return i >= 0 ? args[i + 1] : fallback;
19: };
20: const apply = args.includes("--apply");
21: const itemType = flag("type", "PACKING_MATERIAL");
22: 
23: const envPath = new URL("../.env", import.meta.url).pathname;
24: let dbPath = flag("db", null);
25: if (!dbPath) {
26:   const line = readFileSync(envPath, "utf8")
27:     .split("\n")
28:     .find((l) => l.startsWith("ERP_LOCAL_DB="));
29:   dbPath = (line?.split("=")[1] ?? "").trim().replace(/^\.\//, "");
30:   dbPath = new URL(`../${dbPath}`, import.meta.url).pathname;
31: }
32: 
33: /** Zero-cost batches that still hold stock, with the item's latest purchase price. */
34: function plan(db, type) {
35:   const batches = db
36:     .prepare(
37:       `SELECT sb.id, sb.batch_number, sb.quantity_in_stock AS qty,
38:               sb.purchase_price, sb.raw_unit_cost, sb.packing_unit_cost,
39:               i.id AS item_id, i.item_code, i.item_name
40:          FROM stock_batches sb
41:          JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id
42:         WHERE sb.is_active = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock > 0
43:           AND sb.purchase_price = 0
44:           AND (sb.raw_unit_cost + sb.packing_unit_cost) = 0
45:           AND i.item_type = ?
46:         ORDER BY i.item_name, sb.id`,
47:     )
48:     .all(type);
49: 
50:   const price = db.prepare(
51:     `SELECT pii.unit_cost AS cost, pi.invoice_number AS invoice, pi.invoice_date AS date
52:        FROM purchase_invoice_items pii
53:        JOIN purchase_invoices pi ON pi.id = pii.invoice_id
54:       WHERE pii.item_id = ? AND pii.unit_cost > 0
55:       ORDER BY date(pi.invoice_date) DESC, pii.id DESC
56:       LIMIT 1`,
57:   );
58: 
59:   // A made item has no purchase price: take what the production and packing
60:   // runs cost per unit instead.
61:   const made = db.prepare(
62:     `SELECT ROUND(SUM(cost) / NULLIF(SUM(qty), 0), 4) AS cost, SUM(runs) AS runs
63:        FROM (
64:          SELECT SUM(po.production_cost) AS cost, SUM(po.actual_quantity) AS qty, COUNT(*) AS runs
65:            FROM production_orders po
66:            JOIN bill_of_materials b ON b.id = po.bom_id
67:           WHERE b.finished_item_id = ? AND po.actual_quantity > 0 AND po.production_cost > 0
68:          UNION ALL
69:          SELECT SUM(po.production_cost), SUM(po.actual_quantity), COUNT(*)
70:            FROM packing_orders po
71:            JOIN bill_of_materials b ON b.id = po.bom_id
72:           WHERE b.finished_item_id = ? AND po.actual_quantity > 0 AND po.production_cost > 0
73:        )`,
74:   );
75: 
76:   return batches.map((batch) => {
77:     const bought = price.get(batch.item_id);
78:     if (bought) return { ...batch, price: { ...bought, source: "purchase" } };
79:     const run = made.get(batch.item_id, batch.item_id);
80:     if (run?.cost > 0) {
81:       return {
82:         ...batch,
83:         price: { cost: run.cost, invoice: `${run.runs} run(s)`, date: "production", source: "production" },
84:       };
85:     }
86:     return { ...batch, price: null };
87:   });
88: }
89: 
90: const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath);
91: const rows = plan(db, itemType);
92: 
93: let priced = 0;
94: let pricedQty = 0;
95: let pricedValue = 0;
96: let unpriced = 0;
97: let unpricedQty = 0;

// Dump the tables the cleanup scripts touch, before they touch them.
//
//   node scripts/snapshot-db.mjs                    # testdb -> backups/testdb-<ts>/
//   node scripts/snapshot-db.mjs --allow-main       # main   -> backups/main-<ts>/
//   node scripts/snapshot-db.mjs --url data/e2e.sqlite --out /tmp/snap
//
// One NDJSON file per table plus manifest.json with row counts. Restore is
// manual; this is the undo record, not a restore tool.

import { readFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const webRoot = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");

const TABLES = [
  "bill_of_materials",
  "bom_components",
  "production_orders",
  "production_consumption",
  "packing_orders",
  "packing_consumption",
  "journal_entries",
  "journal_entry_lines",
  "stock_batches",
  "stock_movements",
  "items",
  "numbering_sequences",
];

function envValue(key) {
  const file = path.join(webRoot, ".env");
  if (!existsSync(file)) return "";
  const line = readFileSync(file, "utf8")
    .split(/\r?\n/)
    .find((raw) => raw.trim().startsWith(`${key}=`));
  return line ? line.slice(line.indexOf("=") + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "") : "";
}

const mask = (url) =>
  url.replace(/\/\/[^@]*@/, "//***@").replace(/apikey=[^&]+/, "apikey=***");

async function connect(target) {
  if (target.startsWith("sqlitecloud://")) {
    const { Database } = await import("@sqlitecloud/drivers");
    const db = new Database(target);
    return {
      all: async (sql) => {
        const out = await db.sql(sql);
        return Array.isArray(out) ? out : [];
      },
      close: async () => {},
    };
  }
  const { DatabaseSync } = await import("node:sqlite");
  const db = new DatabaseSync(target);
  return {
    all: async (sql) => db.prepare(sql).all(),
    close: async () => db.close(),
  };
}

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const allowMain = args.includes("--allow-main");
const urlFlag = args.indexOf("--url");
const outFlag = args.indexOf("--out");
const explicit = urlFlag >= 0 ? args[urlFlag + 1] : null;

const main = process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL || envValue("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL");
const testdb = process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_TESTDB_URL || envValue("SQLITE_CLOUD_TESTDB_URL");
const target = explicit || (args.includes("--main") ? main : testdb);

if (!target) {
  console.error("No target: set SQLITE_CLOUD_TESTDB_URL in web/.env or pass --url.");
  process.exit(1);
}
if (main && target === main && !allowMain) {
  console.error("Target is SQLITE_CLOUD_URL (main). Pass --allow-main to override.");
  process.exit(1);
}

11: The "add the missing receipts" fix plan
12: Problem: raw/packing materials were consumed in production but never recorded as received — no purchase invoice, no opening stock — so their batches sit negative (Aerosil's −3.6 is one instance). At the time it was 54 negative batches, raw batches net ≈ −3.29M.
13: The decision we landed on:
14: 1. Do NOT add purchase invoices. Party ledgers are already correct, so a purchase invoice would double-hit A/P or cash.
15: 2. Use Items → Opening stock instead — enter the true on-hand quantity + unit cost, leave supplier blank. This creates only a batch + OPENING movement, no AP entry, and no ledger disturbance.
16: 3. GL follow-up: supplier-less opening stock doesn't touch the ledger, so post a Dr Inventory (1200/1210/1220) / Cr Retained Earnings (3100) entry to record the value.
17: 4. Don't double-enter — once added as opening stock, never re-add the same material as a purchase, and don't also run "Fix negative stock" on the same items.
18: 5. After the quantities are entered, re-run the periodic stock correction to clear the remaining negative batches.
19: Related (also pending from that plan):
20: - P0 FG relief: Dr COGS / Cr 1220 for the ~7.9M finished-goods overstatement (only 15 of 102 sales posted cost) — waiting on your 30-Nov FG count.
21: - P2 fill missing item prices via ?unpriced=true.
22: So the "purchase add" plan, corrected, is: enter the missing receipts as supplier-less opening stock (qty + cost), then one Dr Inventory / Cr Retained Earnings reclass.

1: // One-time cutover: pull the SQLite Cloud database into a local SQLite file so
2: // the app can switch to local-primary mode (see DEPLOYMENT.md, "Local-primary
3: // mode"). Read-only against the cloud.
4: //
5: //   node scripts/pull-cloud.mjs                       # -> data/erp.sqlite.pull
6: //   node scripts/pull-cloud.mjs --out data/erp.sqlite # overwrite the live file
7: //   node scripts/pull-cloud.mjs --url sqlitecloud://… --out /tmp/erp.db
8: //
9: // Prints per-table row counts so they can be compared with the cloud.
10: 
11: import { existsSync, readFileSync, rmSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
12: import path from "node:path";
13: import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
14: import { Database } from "@sqlitecloud/drivers";
15: 
16: const webRoot = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
17: 
18: function envValue(key) {
19:   const file = path.join(webRoot, ".env");
20:   if (!existsSync(file)) return "";
21:   const line = readFileSync(file, "utf8")
22:     .split(/\r?\n/)
23:     .find((raw) => raw.trim().startsWith(`${key}=`));
24:   return line ? line.slice(line.indexOf("=") + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "") : "";
25: }
26: 
27: const arg = (name) => {
28:   const i = process.argv.indexOf(`--${name}`);
29:   return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : undefined;
30: };
31: 
32: const mask = (url) => url.replace(/\/\/[^@]*@/, "//***@").replace(/apikey=[^&]+/, "apikey=***");
33: 
34: const url = arg("url") || process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL || envValue("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL");
35: if (!url) {
36:   console.error("No SQLITE_CLOUD_URL (pass --url or set it in web/.env).");
37:   process.exit(1);
38: }
39: 
40: const out = path.resolve(webRoot, arg("out") || "data/erp.sqlite.pull");
41: if (existsSync(out) && !arg("out")) {
42:   console.error(`${out} already exists; pass --out to choose a target (the live file is not overwritten).`);
43:   process.exit(1);
44: }
45: 
46: console.log(`pulling from ${mask(url)}`);
47: const cloud = new Database(url);
48: const sql = (query) => cloud.sql({ query, parameters: [] });
49: 
50: /** node:sqlite accepts primitives and byte arrays; never coerce silently. */
51: function bindable(value) {
52:   if (value === undefined || value === null) return null;
53:   if (typeof value === "boolean") return value ? 1 : 0;
54:   if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number" || typeof value === "bigint") return value;
55:   if (value instanceof Uint8Array) return value;
56:   throw new Error(`Unsupported value type in pull: ${Object.prototype.toString.call(value)}`);
57: }
58: 
59: // One read transaction: a single point in time, not a mix of table moments.
60: await sql("BEGIN");
61: const schema = (await sql(
62:   "SELECT type, name, sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE sql IS NOT NULL AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'",
63: )).map((r) => ({ type: r.type, name: r.name, sql: r.sql }));
64: const tables = schema.filter((e) => e.type === "table").map((e) => e.name);
65: const sequences = await sql("SELECT name, seq FROM sqlite_sequence").catch(() => []);
66: const sequenceRows = Array.isArray(sequences) ? sequences : [];
67: 
68: const data = {};
69: for (const table of tables) {
70:   const t0 = Date.now();
71:   data[table] = await sql(`SELECT * FROM "${table.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`);
72:   console.log(`  ${table}: ${data[table].length} rows (${Date.now() - t0} ms)`);
73: }
74: await sql("ROLLBACK");
75: 
76: console.log(`writing ${out}`);
77: const local = new DatabaseSync(out);
78: try {
79:   local.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF");
80:   local.exec("BEGIN");
81:   for (const entry of schema.filter((e) => e.type === "table")) local.exec(entry.sql);
82:   for (const [table, rows] of Object.entries(data)) {
83:     if (rows.length === 0) continue;
84:     const columns = Object.keys(rows[0]);
85:     const stmt = local.prepare(
86:       `INSERT INTO "${table.replace(/"/g, '""')}" (${columns.map((c) => `"${c.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(", ")}) ` +
87:         `VALUES (${columns.map(() => "?").join(", ")})`,
88:     );
89:     for (const row of rows) stmt.run(...columns.map((c) => bindable(row[c])));
90:   }
91:   for (const entry of schema.filter((e) => e.type !== "table")) local.exec(entry.sql);
92:   const seq = local.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO sqlite_sequence (name, seq) VALUES (?, ?)");
93:   for (const s of sequenceRows) seq.run(String(s.name), Number(s.seq ?? 0));
94:   local.exec("COMMIT");
95: } catch (err) {
96:   try {
97:     local.exec("ROLLBACK");
98:   } catch {}
99:   local.close();
100:   rmSync(out, { force: true });
101:   throw err;
102: }
103: local.close();
104: await new Promise((r) => cloud.close(r));
105: 
106: const check = new DatabaseSync(out);
107: const integrity = Object.values(check.prepare("PRAGMA integrity_check").get() ?? {})[0];
108: let counts = 0;
109: for (const table of tables) counts += Number(check.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM "${table.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).get().n);
110: check.close();
111: console.log(`integrity_check: ${integrity}`);
112: console.log(`tables: ${tables.length}, rows: ${counts}, bytes: ${statSync(out).size}`);
113: if (integrity !== "ok") {
114:   console.error("Snapshot failed its integrity check; delete the file and retry.");
115:   process.exit(1);
116: }
117: if (counts !== Object.values(data).reduce((s, r) => s + r.length, 0)) {
118:   console.error("Row counts do not match what was read from the cloud; delete the file and retry.");
119:   process.exit(1);
120: }
121: console.log("done. Point ERP_LOCAL_DB at this file and set ERP_DB_ENGINE=local to cut over.");

    item_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES items(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tag_id          INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES item_tags(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    action          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (action IN ('ADD','REMOVE')),
    changed_at      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_item_tag_history_lookup
    ON item_tag_history(item_id, tag_id, changed_at);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_batches (
    id                  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    item_id             INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES items(id),
    warehouse_id        INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES warehouses(id),
    batch_number        TEXT NOT NULL,
    manufacturing_date  TEXT,
    expiry_date         TEXT,
    purchase_price      REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    raw_unit_cost       REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    packing_unit_cost   REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    quantity_in_stock   REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    received_date       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (date('now')),
    is_active           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    UNIQUE (item_id, warehouse_id, batch_number)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_batches_expiry ON stock_batches(expiry_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_batches_item ON stock_batches(item_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_batches_item_warehouse ON stock_batches(item_id, warehouse_id);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_movements (
    id                  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    item_id             INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES items(id),
    batch_id            INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_batches(id),
    warehouse_id        INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES warehouses(id),
    movement_type       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (movement_type IN
                          ('PURCHASE','SALE','SALE_RETURN','PURCHASE_RETURN',
                           'PRODUCTION_IN','PRODUCTION_CONSUME','ADJUSTMENT',
                           'EXPIRY','DAMAGE','OPENING')),
    quantity            REAL NOT NULL,
    unit_cost           REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    reference_table     TEXT,
    reference_id        INTEGER,
    movement_date       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    notes               TEXT,
    created_by          INTEGER REFERENCES users(id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_movements_item ON stock_movements(item_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_movements_ref ON stock_movements(reference_table, reference_id);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sales_invoices (
    id                  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    company_id          INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES companies(id),
    warehouse_id        INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES warehouses(id),
    invoice_number      TEXT NOT NULL,
    customer_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES parties(id),
    invoice_date        TEXT NOT NULL,
    payment_type        TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (payment_type IN ('CASH','BANK','CHEQUE','CREDIT')),
    bank_account_id     INTEGER REFERENCES bank_accounts(id),
    subtotal            REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount     REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount          REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount        REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

session-652004f9-b533-4eb4-9527-5d7e8cbad88d.md:5121:  "content": "// Clear priced negative stock batches and re-book the inventory correction.\n//\n//   npx tsx scripts/fix-negative-stock.ts --local                     # dry run\n//   npx tsx scripts/fix-negative-stock.ts --local data/erp.sqlite --apply\n//   ... --date 2026-11-19\n//\n// Four steps, one transaction:\n//   1. Reverse the old OPENING-STOCK-CORR / PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR pair (posted by\n//      post-periodic-stock-correction.ts). Those are the only manual corrections;\n//      the app's own manufacturing/reversal entries are never touched.\n//   2. Bring every *priced* negative batch to 0 and record the missing receipt\n//      as an OPENING movement. Unpriced batches are left alone — there is no\n//      value to book for them.\n//   3. Dr 1200/1210/1220 / Cr 3100 for the opening value just added.\n//   4. Dr 5000 COGS / Cr 1200 + 1210 so each raw/packing account lands on the\n//      counted batch value. Finished goods are NOT trued up here — that needs a\n//      physical count (see P0).\n//\n// Re-running is safe: every entry carries a fixed reference number and the\n// whole run is skipped once OPENING-STOCK-CORR-2 exists.\n\nimport { existsSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport type { JournalLineInput } from \"../server/src/services/accountingService.js\";\n\nconst OLD_REFS = [\"OPENING-STOCK-CORR\", \"PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR\"];\nconst REV_REFS = [\"REV-OPENING-STOCK-CORR\", \"REV-PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR\"];\nconst OPENING_REF = \"OPENING-STOCK-CORR-2\";\nconst ADJUST_REF = \"PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR-2\";\nconst MAX_ADJUSTMENT = 6_000_000;\n\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst apply = args.includes(\"--apply\");\nconst force = args.includes(\"--force\");\nconst dateArg = args.indexOf(\"--date\");\nconst entryDate = dateArg >= 0 ? args[dateArg + 1] : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n\nconst localArg = args.indexOf(\"--local\");\nconst useLocal = localArg >= 0;\nif (useLocal) {\n  process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = \"local\";\n  const target = args[localArg + 1];\n  if (target && !target.startsWith(\"--\")) process.env.ERP_LOCAL_DB = target;\n}\n\n/** Batch cost when it has one, else the item's purchase price. */\nconst UNIT_COST = \"COALESCE(NULLIF(sb.purchase_price,0), i.purchase_price, 0)\";\n\n/** Inventory account per item type — mirrors INVENTORY_ACCOUNT_BY_TYPE. */\nconst ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE: Record<string, string> = {\n  RAW_MATERIAL: \"1200\",\n  PACKING_MATERIAL: \"1210\",\n  FINISHED_GOOD: \"1220\",\n};\n/** Types the periodic true-up may touch (finished goods wait for the count). */\nconst TRUE_UP_TYPES = [\"RAW_MATERIAL\", \"PACKING_MATERIAL\"];\n\ntype NegativeBatch = {\n  id: number;\n  item_id: number;\n  warehouse_id: number;\n  item_type: string;\n  batch_number: string;\n  quantity: number;\n  price: number;\n};\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  const { getDb } = await import(\"../server/src/db/index.js\");\n  const { env } = await import(\"../server/src/env.js\");\n  const { AccountingService } = await import(\"../server/src/services/accountingService.js\");\n\n  const db = getDb();\n  console.log(\n    `Database: ${\n      useLocal ? `${env.localDbPath} (local — the service must be stopped)` : \"(cloud)\"\n    }  date: ${entryDate}`,\n  );\n\n  const existing = new Set(\n    (\n      await db.all<{ reference_no: string }>(\n        \"SELECT reference_no FROM journal_entries WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no IS NOT NULL\",\n      )\n    ).map((r) => r.reference_no),\n  );\n  if (existing.has(OPENING_REF)) {\n    console.log(`${OPENING_REF} already posted — nothing to do.`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const accountIds = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const row of await db.all<{ id: number; account_code: string }>(\n    \"SELECT id, account_code FROM accounts WHERE company_id = 1 AND account_code IN ('1200','1210','1220','3100','5000')\",\n  )) {\n    accountIds.set(row.account_code, row.id);\n  }\n  const account = (code: string): number => {\n    const id = accountIds.get(code);\n    if (!id) throw new Error(`Account ${code} not found.`);\n    return id;\n  };\n\n  // --- 1. reversal of the old correction pair -------------------------------\n  type Job = { ref: string; narration: string; lines: JournalLineInput[]; total: number };\n  const jobs: Job[] = [];\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < OLD_REFS.length; i += 1) {\n    if (existing.has(REV_REFS[i])) continue;\n    const entry = await db.get<{ id: number; reference_no: string; narration: string | null }>(\n      \"SELECT id, reference_no, narration FROM journal_entries WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no = ?\",\n      [OLD_REFS[i]],\n    );\n    if (!entry) continue;\n    const lines = await db.all<{ account_id: number; debit: number; credit: number; description: string | null }>(\n      \"SELECT account_id, debit, credit, description FROM journal_entry_lines WHERE journal_entry_id = ? ORDER BY id\",\n      [entry.id],\n    );\n    const total = lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Number(l.debit), 0);\n    jobs.push({\n      ref: REV_REFS[i],\n      narration: `Reversal - ${entry.reference_no}`,\n      total,\n      lines: lines.map((l) => ({\n        accountId: l.account_id,\n        ...(Number(l.credit) > 0 ? { debit: Number(l.credit) } : { credit: Number(l.debit) }),\n        description: `Reversal - ${l.description ?? entry.reference_no}`,\n      })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --- 2. priced negative batches -> 0 -------------------------------------\n  const negatives = await db.all<NegativeBatch>(\n    `SELECT sb.id, sb.item_id, sb.warehouse_id, i.item_type, sb.batch_number,\n            ROUND(sb.quantity_in_stock, 4) quantity, ${UNIT_COST} price\n     FROM stock_batches sb\n     JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id\n     WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock < 0 AND ${UNIT_COST} > 0\n     ORDER BY sb.id`,\n  );\n  const skipped = await db.get<{ n: number }>(\n    `SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM stock_batches sb JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id\n     WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock < 0 AND ${UNIT_COST} <= 0`,\n  );\n\n  const openingByType = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const b of negatives) {\n    const code = ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE[b.item_type];\n    if (!code) throw new Error(`No inventory account for item type ${b.item_type}.`);\n    openingByType.set(\n      b.item_type,\n      (openingByType.get(b.item_type) ?? 0) + Math.abs(b.quantity) * b.price,\n    );\n  }\n  const round2 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;\n  for (const [type, v] of openingByType) openingByType.set(type, round2(v));\n  const openingTotal = round2([...openingByType.values()].reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0));\n\n  // --- 3. balances and counted values (after the opening value is added) ----\n  const balances = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const row of await db.all<{ account_code: string; net: number }>(\n    `SELECT a.account_code, ROUND(SUM(jel.debit - jel.credit), 2) net\n     FROM journal_entry_lines jel\n     JOIN accounts a ON a.id = jel.account_id\n     JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jel.journal_entry_id\n     WHERE je.company_id = 1 AND a.account_code IN ('1200','1210','1220')\n     GROUP BY a.id`,\n  )) {\n    balances.set(row.account_code, Number(row.net));\n  }\n  const counted = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const row of await db.all<{ item_type: string; v: number }>(\n    `SELECT i.item_type, ROUND(SUM(sb.quantity_in_stock * ${UNIT_COST}), 2) v\n     FROM stock_batches sb JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id\n     WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock > 0 AND sb.is_active = 1\n     GROUP BY i.item_type`,\n  )) {\n    counted.set(row.item_type, Number(row.v));\n  }\n\n  // The opening entry is posted before the true-up, so its value counts as part\n  // of the balance the true-up has to move; it is also part of the batch value.\n  const plan = Object.values(ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE).map((code) => {\n    const type = Object.keys(ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE).find((t) => ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE[t] === code)!;\n    const balance = balances.get(code) ?? 0;\n    const opening = openingByType.get(type) ?? 0;\n    const target = counted.get(type) ?? 0;\n    return { code, type, balance, opening, target, delta: round2(balance + opening - target) };\n  });\n\n  if (openingTotal > 0) {\n    jobs.push({\n      ref: OPENING_REF,\n      total: openingTotal,\n      narration: \"Opening stock for negative batches brought into the inventory accounts\",\n      lines: plan\n        .filter((p) => p.opening > 0)\n        .map((p) => ({\n          accountId: account(p.code),\n          debit: p.opening,\n          description: `Opening stock — ${p.type.toLowerCase().replace(\"_\", \" \")}`,\n        }))\n        .concat([{ accountId: account(\"3100\"), credit: openingTotal, description: \"Opening stock\" }]),\n    });\n  }\n\n  const tuneUp = plan.filter((p) => TRUE_UP_TYPES.includes(p.type) && Math.abs(p.delta) >= 0.01);\n  const adjustTotal = round2(tuneUp.reduce((s, p) => s + p.delta, 0));\n  if (tuneUp.length > 0) {\n    const lines: JournalLineInput[] = [\n      adjustTotal > 0\n        ? { accountId: account(\"5000\"), debit: adjustTotal, description: \"Materials consumed\" }\n        : {\n            accountId: account(\"5000\"),\n            credit: -adjustTotal,\n            description: \"Materials consumption reversed — stock over the books\",\n          },\n      ...tuneUp.map((p) => ({\n        accountId: account(p.code),\n        ...(p.delta > 0 ? { credit: p.delta } : { debit: -p.delta }),\n        description: `Consumed ${p.type.toLowerCase().replace(\"_\", \" \")}`,\n      })),\n    ];\n    jobs.push({\n      ref: ADJUST_REF,\n      total: adjustTotal,\n      narration: \"Raw and packing materials consumed — opening + purchases less closing stock\",\n      lines,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --- report ---------------------------------------------------------------\n  console.log(`\\n  priced negative batches -> 0 : ${negatives.length}`);\n  console.log(`  unpriced negative batches   : ${skipped?.n ?? 0} (left alone, no value to book)`);\n  console.log(\"\\n            balance    + opening    - counted   = adjustment\");\n  for (const p of plan) {\n    console.log(\n      `  ${p.code} ${p.type.padEnd(16)} ${p.balance.toFixed(2).padStart(12)} ${p.opening\n        .toFixed(2)\n        .padStart(12)} ${p.target.toFixed(2).padStart(12)} ${p.delta.toFixed(2).padStart(14)}${\n        TRUE_UP_TYPES.includes(p.type) ? \"\" : \"   (not trued up)\"\n      }`,\n    );\n  }\n  if (jobs.length === 0) {\n    console.log(\"\\nNothing to post.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  for (const job of jobs) console.log(`  will post ${job.ref} for ${job.total.toFixed(2)}`);\n\n  if (Math.abs(adjustTotal) > MAX_ADJUSTMENT && !force) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Adjustment ${adjustTotal.toFixed(2)} exceeds the ${MAX_ADJUSTMENT} cap — check the count first (use --force to override).`,\n    );\n  }\n  for (const job of jobs) {\n    const debit = round2(job.lines.reduce((s, l) => s + Number(l.debit ?? 0), 0));\n    const credit = round2(job.lines.reduce((s, l) => s + Number(l.credit ?? 0), 0));\n    if (debit !== credit) {\n      throw new Error(`${job.ref} is unbalanced: debit ${debit} vs credit ${credit}.`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!apply) {\n    console.log(\"\\nDry run — nothing written. Re-run with --apply to post.\");\n    return;\n  }\n\n  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const inside = new AccountingService(tx);\n\n    // Reverse first, so the true-up below sees the post-reversal balances.\n    for (const job of jobs.filter((j) => REV_REFS.includes(j.ref))) {\n      const id = await inside.postJournalEntry({\n        voucherType: \"JOURNAL\",\n        entryDate,\n        narration: job.narration,\n        referenceNo: job.ref,\n        lines: job.lines,\n      });\n      // Same marker reverseManualJournal uses, so the UI cannot reverse it twice.\n      const original = OLD_REFS[REV_REFS.indexOf(job.ref)];\n      await tx.run(\n        \"UPDATE journal_entries SET source_table = 'journal_entries', source_id = ? WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no = ?\",\n        [id, original],\n      );\n      console.log(`Posted ${job.ref} as entry #${id} on ${entryDate}.`);\n    }\n\n    for (const b of negatives) {\n      await tx.run(\"UPDATE stock_batches SET quantity_in_stock = 0 WHERE id = ?\", [b.id]);\n      await tx.run(\n        `INSERT INTO stock_movements\n           (item_id, batch_id, warehouse_id, movement_type, quantity, unit_cost,\n            reference_table, movement_date, notes)\n         VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'OPENING', ?, ?, 'negative_stock_fix', ?, ?)`,\n        [\n          b.item_id,\n          b.id,\n          b.warehouse_id,\n          Math.abs(b.quantity),\n          b.price,\n          entryDate,\n          `Missing receipt for negative batch ${b.batch_number}`,\n        ],\n      );\n    }\n    console.log(`Cleared ${negatives.length} priced negative batches and recorded their OPENING movements.`);\n\n    for (const job of jobs.filter((j) => !REV_REFS.includes(j.ref))) {\n      const id = await inside.postJournalEntry({\n        voucherType: \"JOURNAL\",\n        entryDate,\n        narration: job.narration,\n        referenceNo: job.ref,\n        lines: job.lines,\n      });\n      console.log(`Posted ${job.ref} as entry #${id} on ${entryDate}.`);\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nmain()\n  .then(async () => {\n    const { closeDb } = await import(\"../server/src/db/index.js\");\n    await closeDb();\n    process.exit(0);\n  })\n  .catch(async (err) => {\n    console.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n"

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_parties_type ON parties(party_type);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_parties_name ON parties(name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_parties_company_type ON parties(company_id, party_type);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS item_categories (
    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    company_id      INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES companies(id),
    name            TEXT NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (company_id, name)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS items (
    id                  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    company_id          INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES companies(id),
    item_code           TEXT NOT NULL,
    item_name           TEXT NOT NULL,
    generic_name        TEXT,
    formula             TEXT,
    strength            TEXT,
    dosage_form         TEXT,
    unit                TEXT NOT NULL,
    manufacturer        TEXT,
    category_id         INTEGER REFERENCES item_categories(id),
    item_type           TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'FINISHED_GOOD'
                          CHECK (item_type IN ('RAW_MATERIAL','PACKING_MATERIAL','FINISHED_GOOD')),
    purchase_price      REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    selling_price       REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    minimum_stock       REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    maximum_stock       REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tax_rate_id         INTEGER REFERENCES tax_rates(id),
    notes               TEXT,
    is_active           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    UNIQUE (company_id, item_code)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_items_name ON items(item_name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_items_generic ON items(generic_name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_items_company_active ON items(company_id, is_active);

-- Free-form tags (e.g. "bottle") assigned to any number of items, independent
-- of item_type. item_categories/category_id above are legacy and unused.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS item_tags (
    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    company_id      INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES companies(id),
    name            TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    UNIQUE (company_id, name)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS item_tag_links (
    item_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES items(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tag_id          INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES item_tags(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    PRIMARY KEY (item_id, tag_id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_item_tag_links_tag ON item_tag_links(tag_id);

-- Append-only assignment history, so a report can rebuild which tags an item
-- carried on a given date. item_tag_links remains the current state.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS item_tag_history (
    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    item_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES items(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
---
      `SELECT * FROM stock_batches
       WHERE item_id = ? AND warehouse_id = ? AND batch_number = ?`,
      [itemId, warehouseId, batchNumber],
    );
  }

  /** Apply a signed change to a batch's on-hand quantity. */
  async adjustQuantity(batchId: number, delta: number): Promise<void> {
    await this.db.run(
      "UPDATE stock_batches SET quantity_in_stock = quantity_in_stock + ? WHERE id = ?",
      [delta, batchId],
    );
  }

  /** Add production output to a batch and refresh its unit costs. */
  async addToBatch(
    batchId: number,
    quantity: number,
    costs: { purchasePrice: number; rawUnitCost: number; packingUnitCost: number },
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.db.run(
      `UPDATE stock_batches
       SET quantity_in_stock = quantity_in_stock + ?,
           purchase_price = ?, raw_unit_cost = ?, packing_unit_cost = ?
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [quantity, costs.purchasePrice, costs.rawUnitCost, costs.packingUnitCost, batchId],
    );
  }

  async insert(data: InsertData): Promise<number> {
    return insertRow(this.db, "stock_batches", data);
  }
}

2: // written but the first version of the feature did not.

6: // reset line, dated now, so the movement ledger matches the batch ledger again.

1: /**
2:  * Reversible "zero out the stock ledger".
3:  *
4:  * Zeroing sets every batch quantity to 0 but first records the exact quantity
5:  * (and cost) each batch held, so the whole reset can be undone later with a
6:  * delta update. Deliberately stock-only: no journal entry is posted, because
7:  * the accounting side of a stock rewrite is a separate, dated adjustment the
8:  * operator has to make explicitly. The reset header keeps the name of the
9:  * database snapshot taken immediately before it ran.
10:  */
11: import type { SqlDatabase } from "../db/types.js";

17: } from "../repositories/stockLedgerRepository.js";

19: export interface ZeroStockLedgerInput {
20:   reason?: string | null;
21:   createdBy?: number | null;
22:   /** Snapshot written just before the reset (the route owns the backup service). */
23:   snapshotFile?: string | null;
24: }
25: 
26: export interface ZeroStockLedgerResult {
27:   resetId: number;
28:   batchCount: number;
29:   totalQuantity: number;
30:   totalValue: number;
31:   snapshotFile: string | null;
32: }

35:   resetId: number;
36:   batchCount: number;
37:   restoredAt: string;

40: export class StockLedgerService {
41:   private readonly resets: StockLedgerRepository;
42: 
43:   constructor(
44:     private readonly db: SqlDatabase,
45:     private readonly companyId = 1,
46:   ) {
47:     this.resets = new StockLedgerRepository(db);
48:   }
49: 
50:   /** Reset history, newest first. */
51:   async list(): Promise<StockLedgerResetRow[]> {
52:     return this.resets.list(this.companyId);
53:   }
54: 
55:   /** Zero every batch quantity, recording what was removed so it can be undone. */
56:   async zero(input: ZeroStockLedgerInput = {}): Promise<ZeroStockLedgerResult> {
57:     const batches = await this.resets.listBatches(this.companyId);
58:     if (batches.length === 0) {
59:       throw new ValidationError("Every stock batch is already at zero.");
60:     }
61: 
62:     let totalQuantity = new Decimal(0);
63:     let totalValue = new Decimal(0);
64:     for (const batch of batches) {
65:       const quantity = dec(batch.quantity);
66:       totalQuantity = totalQuantity.plus(quantity);
67:       totalValue = totalValue.plus(quantity.times(dec(batch.unit_cost)));
68:     }
69:     const reason = input.reason?.trim() ? input.reason.trim() : null;
70: 
71:     return this.db.transaction(async () => {
72:       const resetId = await this.resets.insertReset({
73:         company_id: this.companyId,
74:         reason,
75:         batch_count: batches.length,
76:         total_quantity: toQuantity(totalQuantity),
77:         total_value: toStorage(round2(totalValue)),
78:         snapshot_file: input.snapshotFile ?? null,
79:         created_by: input.createdBy ?? null,
80:       });
81:       for (const batch of batches) {
82:         await this.resets.insertLine(resetId, batch);
83:         await this.db.run("UPDATE stock_batches SET quantity_in_stock = 0 WHERE id = ?", [
84:           batch.batch_id,
85:         ]);
86:         // Offset the batch in the movement ledger too, otherwise on-hand and
87:         // SUM(stock_movements) disagree for every batch this reset touched.
88:         await this.resets.insertAdjustment({
89:           itemId: batch.item_id,
90:           batchId: batch.batch_id,
91:           warehouseId: batch.warehouse_id,
92:           quantity: -batch.quantity,
93:           unitCost: batch.unit_cost,
94:           resetId,
95:           notes: `Stock ledger zeroed (reset #${resetId})`,
96:           createdBy: input.createdBy ?? null,
97:         });
98:       }
99:       return {
100:         resetId,
101:         batchCount: batches.length,
102:         totalQuantity: toQuantity(totalQuantity),
103:         totalValue: toStorage(round2(totalValue)),
104:         snapshotFile: input.snapshotFile ?? null,
105:       };
106:     });
107:   }
108: 
109:   /**
110:    * Undo a reset by adding each batch's recorded quantity back (a delta, so it
111:    * is the exact inverse of the zero). Stock added after the reset lives in
112:    * other batches and is untouched — but it does mean a restore after loading
113:    * new stock counts the old batches again, which the UI warns about.
114:    */
115:   async restore(
116:     resetId: number,
117:     restoredBy: number | null = null,
118:   ): Promise<RestoreStockLedgerResult> {
119:     const reset = await this.resets.findById(resetId, this.companyId);
120:     if (!reset) throw new NotFoundError(`Stock ledger reset ${resetId} not found.`);
121:     if (reset.restored_at) {
122:       throw new ValidationError(`This reset was already restored on ${reset.restored_at}.`);
123:     }
124:     const lines = await this.resets.lines(resetId);
125:     if (lines.length === 0) {
126:       throw new ValidationError("This reset has no batch lines to restore.");
127:     }
128: 
129:     return this.db.transaction(async () => {
130:       for (const line of lines) {
131:         await this.db.run(
132:           "UPDATE stock_batches SET quantity_in_stock = quantity_in_stock + ? WHERE id = ?",
133:           [line.quantity, line.batch_id],
134:         );
135:         await this.resets.insertAdjustment({
136:           itemId: line.item_id,
137:           batchId: line.batch_id,
138:           warehouseId: line.warehouse_id,
139:           quantity: line.quantity,
140:           unitCost: line.unit_cost,
141:           resetId,
142:           notes: `Stock ledger restored (reset #${resetId})`,
143:           createdBy: restoredBy,
144:         });
145:       }
146:       const updated = await this.resets.markRestored(resetId, this.companyId, restoredBy);
147:       return {
148:         resetId,
149:         batchCount: lines.length,
150:         restoredAt: updated?.restored_at ?? "",
151:       };
152:     });
153:   }
154: }

{
  "command": "node -e '\nconst { DatabaseSync } = require(\"node:sqlite\");\nconst { Database } = require(\"@sqlitecloud/drivers\");\nconst url = \"sqlitecloud://cjja8z6pvz.g4.sqlite.cloud:8860/fixed?apikey=bmJZ0l1RTFCoxS0Au17c0iofzZmrDn2Db94v0YtV9Uw\";\n(async () => {\n  const local = new DatabaseSync(\"data/fixed-copy.sqlite\", { readOnly: true });\n  const cloud = new Database(url);\n  const q = async (s) => await cloud.sql({ query: s, parameters: [] });\n  const li = new Map(local.prepare(\"SELECT id, purchase_price FROM items\").all().map(r => [r.id, r.purchase_price]));\n  const ci = await q(\"SELECT id, purchase_price FROM items\");\n  let changed = 0, newItems = 0, examples = [];\n  for (const r of ci) {\n    if (!li.has(r.id)) { newItems++; continue; }\n    if (Math.abs(Number(li.get(r.id)) - Number(r.purchase_price)) > 0.0001) { changed++; if (examples.length<12) examples.push({id:r.id, before:li.get(r.id), after:r.purchase_price}); }\n  }\n  console.log(\"items price changed since Nov16:\", changed, \"new items:\", newItems);\n  console.log(examples);\n  const lb = new Map(local.prepare(\"SELECT id, purchase_price, raw_unit_cost, packing_unit_cost, quantity_in_stock FROM stock_batches\").all().map(r => [r.id, r]));\n  const cb = await q(\"SELECT id, purchase_price, raw_unit_cost, packing_unit_cost, quantity_in_stock FROM stock_batches\");\n  let bchanged = 0, bnew = 0, bex = [];\n  for (const r of cb) {\n    if (!lb.has(r.id)) { bnew++; continue; }\n    const b = lb.get(r.id);\n    if (Math.abs(Number(b.purchase_price) - Number(r.purchase_price)) > 0.0001) { bchanged++; if (bex.length<10) bex.push({id:r.id,before:b.purchase_price,after:r.purchase_price,qty:r.quantity_in_stock}); }\n  }\n  console.log(\"batch purchase_price changed since Nov16:\", bchanged, \"new batches:\", bnew);\n  console.log(bex);\n  local.close(); await new Promise(r => cloud.close(r));\n})().catch(e => { console.error(e.message); process.exit(1); });\n'",
  "timeout": 120000,
  "workdir": "/home/oni/Documents/projects/work/BOP-software/web"
}

      `  ${row.order.order_number.padEnd(8)}  ${row.order.manufacturing_date}  ${String(row.actual).padStart(6)}  ` +
        `${String(row.oldBags).padStart(6)} -> ${String(row.expected).padEnd(6)}  ${String(row.delta).padStart(6)}  ` +
        `${row.value.toFixed(2).padStart(8)}  ${String(row.stock).padStart(6)}  ${String(row.sold).padStart(4)}  ${String(row.removed).padStart(7)}`,
    );
  }
  console.log(`\n  orders: ${plan.length}`);
  console.log(`  extra bags: ${q(plan.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.delta, 0))} @ ${bagUnitCost}`);
  console.log(`  Journal: Dr Finished Goods ${totalStock.toFixed(2)}, Dr COGS ${totalCogs.toFixed(2)}, Cr Packing Material ${total.toFixed(2)}`);

  if (total > MAX_TOTAL) {
    throw new Error(`Total ${total.toFixed(2)} exceeds the ${MAX_TOTAL} safety cap; refusing.`);
  }

  const existing = await db.get("SELECT id FROM journal_entries WHERE reference_no = ?", [REFERENCE]);
  if (existing) {
    console.log(`\nCorrection journal already posted as entry #${existing.id}. Nothing to do.`);
    await closeDb();
    return;
  }

  if (!apply) {
    console.log("\nDry run — nothing written. Re-run with --apply.");
    await closeDb();
    return;
  }

  const accounts = await db.all(
    "SELECT id, account_code FROM accounts WHERE company_id = 1 AND account_code IN ('1210','1220','5000')",
  );
  const account = (code) => {
    const found = accounts.find((row) => row.account_code === code);
    if (!found) throw new Error(`Account ${code} not found.`);
    return found.id;
  };

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    for (const row of plan) {
      const order = row.order;

      await tx.run("UPDATE packing_orders SET production_cost = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", [
        money(n(order.production_cost) + row.value),
        new Date().toISOString(),
        order.id,
      ]);

      if (order.batch_id != null) {
        const packingPerUnit = n(order.packing_unit_cost) + row.value / row.actual;
        const rawPerUnit = n(order.raw_unit_cost);
        await tx.run(
          "UPDATE stock_batches SET packing_unit_cost = ?, purchase_price = ? WHERE id = ?",
          [money(packingPerUnit), money(rawPerUnit + packingPerUnit), order.batch_id],
        );
      }

      if (row.batchId != null) {
        await tx.run(
          "UPDATE packing_consumption SET quantity_consumed = ? WHERE packing_order_id = ? AND component_item_id = ?",
          [row.expected, order.id, bagItem.id],
        );
        const batch = await tx.get(
          "SELECT quantity_in_stock FROM stock_batches WHERE id = ?",
          [row.batchId],
        );
        await tx.run("UPDATE stock_batches SET quantity_in_stock = ? WHERE id = ?", [
          q(n(batch?.quantity_in_stock) - row.delta),
          row.batchId,
        ]);
        await tx.run(
          `INSERT INTO stock_movements
             (item_id, batch_id, warehouse_id, movement_type, quantity, unit_cost,
              reference_table, reference_id, movement_date, notes)
           VALUES (?, ?, 1, 'PRODUCTION_CONSUME', ?, ?, 'packing_orders', ?, ?, ?)`,

32:     .split(/\r?\n/)
33:     .find((raw) => raw.trim().startsWith(`${key}=`));
34:   return line ? line.slice(line.indexOf("=") + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "") : "";
35: }
36: 
37: const arg = (name) => {
38:   const i = process.argv.indexOf(`--${name}`);
39:   return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : undefined;
40: };
41: const flag = (name) => process.argv.includes(`--${name}`);
42: 
43: const mask = (url) => url.replace(/\/\/[^@]*@/, "//***@").replace(/apikey=[^&]+/, "apikey=***");
44: const stamp = () => new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, "").replace(/\..+$/, "").replace("T", "_");
45: 
46: const dbFile = path.resolve(
47:   webRoot,
48:   arg("db") || process.env.ERP_LOCAL_DB || envValue("ERP_LOCAL_DB") || "data/erp.sqlite",
49: );
50: const url = arg("url") || process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL || envValue("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL");
51: const dryRun = flag("dry-run");
52: 
53: if (!url) {
54:   console.error("No SQLITE_CLOUD_URL (pass --url or set it in web/.env).");
55:   process.exit(1);
56: }
57: if (!existsSync(dbFile)) {
58:   console.error(`Local database not found: ${dbFile}`);
59:   process.exit(1);
60: }
61: 
62: console.log(`pushing ${dbFile} -> ${mask(url)}${dryRun ? " (dry run)" : ""}`);
63: 
64: const SCHEMA_SQL =
65:   "SELECT type, name, sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE sql IS NOT NULL AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'";
66: 
67: const tempDir = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), "bop-push-"));
68: const snapshot = path.join(tempDir, `snapshot-${stamp()}.db`);
69: 
70: function readPlan(file) {
71:   const local = new DatabaseSync(file);
72:   try {
73:     const schema = local.prepare(SCHEMA_SQL).all();
74:     const tables = schema.filter((entry) => entry.type === "table").map((entry) => entry.name);
75:     const fkDeps = {};
76:     for (const table of tables) {
77:       const refs = local.prepare(`PRAGMA foreign_key_list(${quoteIdent(table)})`).all();
78:       fkDeps[table] = [...new Set(refs.map((fk) => fk.table))];
79:     }
80:     const data = {};
81:     for (const table of tables) {
82:       data[table] = local.prepare(`SELECT * FROM ${quoteIdent(table)}`).all();
83:     }
84:     return buildPushPlan(schema, data, fkDeps);
85:   } finally {
86:     local.close();
87:   }
88: }
89: 
90: function execAsync(db, sql) {
91:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
92:     try {
93:       db.exec(sql, (err) => (err ? reject(err) : resolve()));
94:     } catch (err) {
95:       reject(err);
96:     }
97:   });
98: }
99: 
100: function sqlAsync(db, sql) {
101:   return db.sql({ query: sql, parameters: [] });
102: }
103: 
104: function closeAsync(db) {
105:   return new Promise((resolve) => {
106:     try {
107:       db.close(() => resolve());
108:     } catch {
109:       resolve();
110:     }
111:   });
112: }
113: 
114: let plan;
115: try {
116:   // One consistent point-in-time snapshot even if the file is in WAL mode.
117:   const local = new DatabaseSync(dbFile);
118:   try {
119:     local.exec(`VACUUM INTO '${snapshot.replace(/'/g, "''")}'`);
120:   } finally {
121:     local.close();
122:   }
123:   plan = readPlan(snapshot);
124: } catch (err) {
125:   rmSync(tempDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
126:   console.error(`could not read ${dbFile}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : err}`);
127:   process.exit(1);
128: }
129: 
130: try {
131:   console.log(
132:     `  ${plan.tables} tables, ${plan.rows} rows, ${(plan.bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB in ` +
133:       `${plan.chunks.length} chunk(s), snapshot ${(statSync(snapshot).size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`,
134:   );
135:   if (dryRun) {
136:     console.log("dry run: nothing sent");
137:     process.exit(0);
138:   }
139: 
140:   const started = Date.now();
141:   const cloud = new Database(url);
142:   try {
143:     for (const chunk of plan.chunks) await execAsync(cloud, chunk);
144:     const tables = Object.keys(plan.counts);
145:     if (tables.length > 0) {
146:       const rows = await sqlAsync(cloud, buildCountQuery(tables));
147:       const actual = rows[0] ?? {};
148:       for (const [table, expected] of Object.entries(plan.counts)) {
149:         const got = Number(actual[table] ?? -1);
150:         if (got !== expected) {
151:           throw new Error(`Cloud table '${table}' has ${got} rows, expected ${expected}.`);
152:         }
153:       }
154:     }
155:     console.log(
156:       `pushed ${plan.rows} rows to SQLite Cloud in ${((Date.now() - started) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`,
157:     );
158:   } catch (err) {
159:     try {
160:       await execAsync(cloud, "ROLLBACK");
161:     } catch {
162:       // the original failure is the useful one
163:     }
164:     throw err;
165:   } finally {
166:     await closeAsync(cloud);
167:   }
168: } finally {
169:   rmSync(tempDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
170: }

{

  "timeout": 120000,
  "workdir": "/home/oni/Documents/projects/work/BOP-software/web"
}

  /**
   * Return every consumption row of an order to stock. The order's consume
   * movements are removed rather than reversed, so the batches it drew from
   * are free again (same as undoing a return or an invoice).
   */
  private async returnConsumptions(
    table: string,
    orderColumn: string,
    orderId: number,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const rows = await this.db.all<{ batch_id: number; component_item_id: number; quantity_consumed: number; unit_cost: number }>(
      `SELECT batch_id, component_item_id, quantity_consumed, unit_cost FROM ${table} WHERE ${orderColumn} = ?`,
      [orderId],
    );
    const stage = table === "production_consumption" ? "production" : "packing";
    for (const row of rows) {
      if (!row.batch_id) continue;
      await this.batches.adjustQuantity(
        Number(row.batch_id),
        toQuantity(dec(row.quantity_consumed)),
      );
    }
    await this.db.run(
      `DELETE FROM stock_movements
       WHERE reference_table = ? AND reference_id = ? AND movement_type = 'PRODUCTION_CONSUME'`,
      [`${stage}_orders`, orderId],
    );
  }

  /**
   * Undo an order's PRODUCTION_IN movements: each output batch must have no
   * live movements (i.e. nothing was sold, packed or adjusted from it), then
   * its movements are removed and the batch is zeroed and deactivated.
   */
  private async removeOutputBatches(
    sourceTable: string,
    orderId: number,
    orderNumber: string,
    force = false,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const outputs = await this.db.all<{ id: number; batch_id: number; quantity: number }>(
      `SELECT id, batch_id, quantity FROM stock_movements
       WHERE reference_table = ? AND reference_id = ? AND movement_type = 'PRODUCTION_IN'`,
      [sourceTable, orderId],
    );
    const batchIds = [...new Set(outputs.map((row) => Number(row.batch_id)))];
    for (const batchId of batchIds) {
      const batch = await this.batches.findById(batchId);
      if (!batch) continue;
      await this.dropUndoneConsumptions(batchId);
      const blocker = await this.db.get<{ reference_table: string | null; reference_id: number | null }>(
        `SELECT reference_table, reference_id FROM stock_movements
         WHERE batch_id = ? AND NOT (reference_table = ? AND reference_id = ? AND movement_type = 'PRODUCTION_IN')
         LIMIT 1`,
        [batchId, sourceTable, orderId],
      );
      if (blocker && !force) {
        throw new ValidationError(
          `Batch '${batch.batch_number}' (${orderNumber}) ${await this.describeBlocker(blocker)} — reopen that order first.`,
        );
      }
      if (blocker) {
        // Force: keep every downstream movement (the pack or sale still refers
        // to this batch) and undo only this order's output. If the batch was
        // already consumed, it is left negative — that is the correction, not
        // an error.
        for (const output of outputs.filter((row) => Number(row.batch_id) === batchId)) {
          await this.db.run("DELETE FROM stock_movements WHERE id = ?", [output.id]);
          await this.batches.adjustQuantity(batchId, -toQuantity(dec(output.quantity)));
        }
        continue;
      }
      await this.db.run("DELETE FROM stock_movements WHERE batch_id = ?", [batchId]);
      await this.batches.adjustQuantity(batchId, -toQuantity(dec(batch.quantity_in_stock)));
      await this.db.run("UPDATE stock_batches SET quantity_in_stock = 0, is_active = 0 WHERE id = ?", [batchId]);
    }
  }

    );

**Target result:** the 90 batches go back to the quantities they would have now if the fix had never run, i.e. `target = −fix_qty + (movements created after the fix)`. That keeps the Nov 20–23 purchases/manufacturing and does not use today's drifted quantities (some were resynced to the movement ledger since the Nov 22 snapshot).

|---|---|---|
| RAW_MATERIAL | 51 | −2,238.16 |
| PACKING_MATERIAL | 33 | −7,661.00 |
| FINISHED_GOOD | 6 | −157.00 |
| **Total** | **90** | **−10,056.16** (all 90 negative) |

Examples: Molasses −443.8, SUPER YEAST −40, PG −41.29, Vitamin A −0.299, BOTTLE ROUND LIT −552, WHITE BAG UNPRINT −733.

**Steps**

1. **Stop the app** on whichever machine writes to `fixed` (currently the cloud is getting manufacturing entries).
2. **Backup both sides**
   - cloud `fixed` → `backups/fixed-before-undo-negfix-<ts>/` (NDJSON snapshot) + a `.db` pull
   - laptop `data/erp.sqlite` → `backups/erp.before-undo-negfix-<ts>.sqlite` (pre-fix `erp.before-fix-20261118_232030.sqlite` already exists)
3. **Dry run** (new `scripts/undo-negative-stock-fix.ts`, modelled on `fix-negative-stock.ts`): prints all 90 rows (current → target), the two reversal entries, and refuses if `REV-OPENING-STOCK-CORR-2` already exists.
4. **Apply in one transaction, on the live DB (`fixed`)**
   - set each of the 90 batches to `target`
   - delete the 90 `negative_stock_fix` OPENING movements (that removes the unit costs you entered)
   - post reversals dated today:
     - `REV-OPENING-STOCK-CORR-2` — Dr 3100 5,686,996.28 / Cr 1200 4,637,532.48, Cr 1210 939,534.00, Cr 1220 109,929.80
     - `REV-PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR-2` — Dr 1200 1,845,014.81, Dr 1210 994,833.46 / Cr 5000 2,839,848.27
     - mark them `source_table='journal_entries'`, `source_id` 1132/1133 so they can't be double-reversed
5. **Sync the laptop**: after the undo, pull `fixed` into the laptop's `data/erp.sqlite` (backup first) so both match. That satisfies "both" without running the undo twice.
6. **Verify**: count 90 batches at target, trial balance debits = credits, 1200/1210/1220 back to pre-fix net + post-Nov-19 activity, `REV-*` refs present.

/**
 * Central configuration, mirroring the Python project's config/app_config.py.
 *
 * Values come from web/.env only. The web app is self-contained: it never
 * reads the Python app's python/.env, so each application can be configured
 * and deployed independently. Nothing else in the codebase reads
 * process.env directly.
 */
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)); // web/server/src
const serverRoot = path.resolve(here, ".."); // web/server
const webRoot = path.resolve(here, "..", ".."); // web (repo root of this app)

function loadDotEnv(file: string): void {
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return;
  for (const raw of fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8").split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const line = raw.trim();
    if (!line || line.startsWith("#") || !line.includes("=")) continue;
    const idx = line.indexOf("=");
    const key = line.slice(0, idx).trim();
    let value = line.slice(idx + 1).trim();
    if (
      (value.startsWith('"') && value.endsWith('"')) ||
      (value.startsWith("'") && value.endsWith("'"))
    ) {
      value = value.slice(1, -1);
    }
    if (process.env[key] === undefined) process.env[key] = value;
  }
}

// web/.env is the only environment file for this application.
loadDotEnv(path.join(webRoot, ".env"));

export type DbEngine = "local" | "sqlitecloud";

function readEngine(): DbEngine {
  const raw = (process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE ?? "local").toLowerCase();
  return raw === "sqlitecloud" ? "sqlitecloud" : "local";
}

/** Backup directory (mirrors the Python app's `backups/` folder). */
const backupDir = path.resolve(webRoot, process.env.ERP_BACKUP_DIR ?? "backups");

/**
 * Auto-backup interval in hours. 0 (the default) disables the scheduler; the
 * Python app defaulted to every 24 hours, so set ERP_AUTO_BACKUP_HOURS=24 to
 * match it. Kept opt-in so a dev machine does not start writing snapshots.
 */
function readAutoBackupHours(): number {
  const raw = Number(process.env.ERP_AUTO_BACKUP_HOURS ?? 0);
  return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw > 0 ? raw : 0;
}

/** `1/true/yes/on` (case-insensitive) mean true; anything else means false. */
function readFlag(raw: string | undefined, fallback = false): boolean {
  if (raw === undefined) return fallback;
  return ["1", "true", "yes", "on"].includes(raw.trim().toLowerCase());
}

/** An optional path resolved against `web/`; unset or blank means "not configured". */
function optionalPath(raw: string | undefined): string | null {
  const value = (raw ?? "").trim();
  return value ? path.resolve(webRoot, value) : null;
}

/**
 * Like `optionalPath`, but keeps a UNC share (`\\\\pc\\share`) untouched.
 * Resolving that against `web/` would produce a path no Windows machine can open.
 */
function optionalDir(raw: string | undefined): string | null {
  const value = (raw ?? "").trim();
  if (!value) return null;
  return /^[\\/]{2}/.test(value) ? value : path.resolve(webRoot, value);
}

const localDbPath = path.resolve(webRoot, process.env.ERP_LOCAL_DB ?? "data/dev.sqlite");

/**
 * Database name used in snapshot file names (`maindb_20261103_120412.db`):
 * the hosted database from `SQLITE_CLOUD_URL`, otherwise the local file name.
 */
function readDbName(): string {
  const raw = (() => {
    if (readEngine() === "sqlitecloud") {
      try {
        return path.basename(new URL(process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL ?? "").pathname);
      } catch {
        return "";
      }
    }
    return path.basename(localDbPath);
  })();
  return raw.replace(/\.(db|sqlite)$/i, "").replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]/g, "-") || "db";
}

/** Named snapshots to keep; 0 disables pruning. */
function readBackupKeep(): number {
  const raw = Number(process.env.ERP_BACKUP_KEEP ?? 7);
  return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw >= 0 ? Math.floor(raw) : 7;
}

/**
 * Second copy of every backup, saved to a folder on this machine's desktop
 * (`<home>/Desktop/desktop`). `ERP_DESKTOP_DIR` overrides it.
 */
function readDesktopDir(): string {
  const value = (process.env.ERP_DESKTOP_DIR ?? "").trim();
  return value ? path.resolve(webRoot, value) : path.join(os.homedir(), "Desktop", "desktop");
}

/** Idle gap before the live copy is rewritten; 0 means immediately. */
function readMirrorDebounceSeconds(): number {
  const raw = Number(process.env.ERP_MIRROR_DEBOUNCE_SECONDS ?? 15);
  return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw >= 0 ? raw : 15;
}

import { env } from "../env.js";
import { ConfigurationError } from "../domain/errors.js";
import { LocalSqliteDatabase } from "./localSqlite.js";
import { SqliteCloudDatabase } from "./sqliteCloud.js";
import type { SqlDatabase } from "./types.js";

/**
 * A database loaded through Settings → Database. When set, the local adapter
 * opens this file instead of `ERP_LOCAL_DB`; the real database file is never
 * touched, so a restart always returns to it. In-memory only: cleared on exit.
 */
let localOverride: { file: string; label: string } | null = null;

/** Point the local engine at another file (or `null` to use `ERP_LOCAL_DB`). */
export function setLocalDbOverride(next: { file: string; label: string } | null): void {
  localOverride = next;
}

/** The hot-swapped database, or `null` when the real local file is active. */
export function getLocalDbOverride(): { file: string; label: string } | null {
  return localOverride;
}

/** Build a database adapter from configuration. */
export function createDatabase(): SqlDatabase {
  if (env.dbEngine === "sqlitecloud") {
    if (!env.sqliteCloudUrl) {
      throw new ConfigurationError(
        "ERP_DB_ENGINE=sqlitecloud but SQLITE_CLOUD_URL is not set.",
      );
    }
    return new SqliteCloudDatabase(env.sqliteCloudUrl);
  }
  return new LocalSqliteDatabase(localOverride?.file ?? env.localDbPath);
}

let instance: SqlDatabase | null = null;

export function getDb(): SqlDatabase {
  if (!instance) instance = createDatabase();
  return instance;
}

export async function closeDb(): Promise<void> {
  if (instance) {
    await instance.close();
    instance = null;
  }
}

export type { SqlDatabase, Row, SqlValue, RunResult } from "./types.js";
export { SCHEMA_SQL } from "./schema.js";

/**
 * SQLite Cloud adapter built on the official `@sqlitecloud/drivers` package.
 *
 * The driver opens a single serialized connection: `sql()` returns an array
 * of rows for SELECTs and a `{ lastID, changes }` context object for writes.
 * Because every command goes to the same connection, BEGIN/COMMIT works, but
 * we still guard the whole transaction with an async lock so a concurrent
 * request cannot interleave statements into the middle of it.
 *
 * `AsyncLocalStorage` makes the lock re-entrant: statements issued *inside* a
 * transaction body bypass the lock (they already hold it), while nested
 * `transaction()` calls simply join the outer one.
 *
 * Remote sockets can drop or idle out. We treat that as a *transient* fault and
 * retry the failed operation once on the same connection object. The driver
 * itself is stateful: a credential or configuration failure will repeat on every
 * attempt, so we bound retries and classify repeated failures as permanent.
 */
import { AsyncLocalStorage } from "node:async_hooks";
import { Database } from "@sqlitecloud/drivers";
import { DatabaseError } from "../domain/errors.js";
import type { Row, RunResult, SqlDatabase, SqlValue } from "./types.js";

const txContext = new AsyncLocalStorage<boolean>();

interface WriteContext {
  changes?: number;
  lastID?: number | bigint;
}

/** Transient socket / idle-timeout faults that are worth one reconnect retry. */
const TRANSIENT_DB_CODES = new Set([
  "ECONNRESET",
  "ECONNREFUSED",
  "ETIMEDOUT",
  "ENOTFOUND",
  "EPIPE",
  "PROTOCOL_CONNECTION_LOST",
  "SQLITE_CLOUD_DISCONNECTED",
  "SQLITE_CLOUD_CONNECTION_LOST",
  "SQLITE_CLOUD_CONNECTION_FAIL",
  "SQLITE_CLOUD_CONNECTION_ERROR",
  "SocketError",
  "NetworkError",
]);

function isTransient(err: unknown): boolean {
  if (err instanceof Error) {
    if (TRANSIENT_DB_CODES.has(err.name)) return true;
    if (TRANSIENT_DB_CODES.has((err as unknown as { code?: string }).code ?? "")) return true;
    const message = err.message ?? "";
    return TRANSIENT_DB_CODES.has(message);
  }
  return false;
}

/** Permanent configuration / credential failures that retry cannot fix. */
function isPermanent(err: unknown): boolean {
  if (err instanceof Error) {
    const name = err.name;
    const code = (err as unknown as { code?: string }).code ?? "";
    if (
      name === "TypeError" ||
      name === "URIError" ||
      name === "SyntaxError" ||
      code === "ECONNREFUSED" ||
      code === "ENOTFOUND" ||
      code === "EINVAL"
    ) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

  private chain: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
